([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-classifier-cell-type-annotation))
- **knn-classifier-cell-type-prediction**: Use when a query scRNA-seq dataset has been mapped into a Symphony reference embedding with mapQuery and you need cell type labels for query cells without retraining from raw reference data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-classifier-cell-type-prediction))
- **knn-classifier-evaluation**: Use when single-cell data has specificity annotation (e.g. pMHC binding labels on a 10x Genomics T cell dataset) and you need to score embedding quality by antigen-specificity prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-classifier-evaluation))
- **knn-correlation-based-neighbor-imputation**: Use when metabolomics datasets contain missing values (from LOD/MNAR or MAR mechanisms) and you need a correlation-distance KNN baseline imputation to benchmark against, or to reproduce KNN-CR arms in the paper's RMSE and MLCI evaluation workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/knn-correlation-based-neighbor-imputation))
- **knn-correlation-mapping-quality-assessment**: Use when a query dataset has been mapped into a reference embedding (e.g., with Symphony mapQuery) and you need a per-cell metric quantifying whether the mapped coordinates reproduce the local neighborhood structure of a de novo integrated gold standard. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-correlation-mapping-quality-assessment))
- **knn-cross-validated-classification-benchmark**: Use when you have a low-dimensional cell embedding (e.g., scPhere 'wn' latent coordinates) plus per-cell labels such as embryo collection time, and need a quantitative time-continuity comparison across embedding methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-cross-validated-classification-benchmark))
- **knn-cross-validation-classification**: Use when you must benchmark the k-NN (k = 5) DEG-feature classifier's malignant vs non-malignant performance (accuracy, sensitivity, specificity, balanced accuracy, AUROC) within the five cancer scRNA-seq training datasets under 10-fold cross-validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-cross-validation-classification))
- **knn-distance-null-distribution-permutation-testing**: Use when projected query cells (e.g. PBMC query mapped onto the DISCO PBMC reference via ProjectNewdata) need a statistical per-cell mapping-quality score before downstream label transfer or filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-distance-null-distribution-permutation-testing))
- **knn-ensemble-cell-classification**: Use when you need to label individual cells in a normalized scRNA-seq matrix (genes as rownames, cells as colnames) as malignant or non-malignant, especially when you have no annotated training cohort of your own, since five pretrained k-NN (k = 5) classifiers are shipped. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-ensemble-cell-classification))
- **knn-ensemble-majority-voting**: Use when classifying cells from a normalized scRNA-seq expression matrix (genes as rows, cells as columns) as cancer vs non-cancer, and per-cell voting frequencies (freq_cancer/freq_non_cancer) are needed for downstream accuracy/sensitivity/AUROC evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-ensemble-majority-voting))
- **knn-ensemble-model-training**: Use when a normalized single-cell expression matrix must be classified as malignant vs non-malignant using PreCanCell's five-dataset k-NN (k=5) ensemble with majority vote over common DEG features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-ensemble-model-training))
- **knn-extrinsic-profile-simulation**: Use when deconvolving spatial transcriptomics data (e.g. 10x Visium spots) with a trained SpatialDDLS model and you want neighbor-aware, spatially regularized cell type proportions for each spot. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-extrinsic-profile-simulation))
- **knn-gcf-classifier-training**: Use when paired genome–MS/MS samples are available and the goal is to classify query MS/MS spectra (reference or cryptic) into candidate GCFs via a k-nearest neighbor model over BGC similarity fingerprints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/knn-gcf-classifier-training))
- **knn-graph-branch-detection**: Use when a pseudotime-ordered single-cell trajectory (e.g., DTFLOW pseudotime with root cell indices) must be split into sub-branches at bifurcation points, and existing tools detect only a single bifurcation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-branch-detection))
- **knn-graph-clustering-hematopoiesis-scrna-seq**: Use when you need to test how sensitive an automated terminal-state/fate-detection method (VIA) is to graph parameters (KNN, ncomps) on a small annotated scRNA-seq hematopoiesis dataset, or to recover rare dendritic lineages and validate them by marker gene trends. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-clustering-hematopoiesis-scrna-seq))
- **knn-graph-construction**: Use when comparing velocity inference methods (e.g., scKINETICS, scVelo, VeloVAE, UniTVelo) on local smoothness of their velocity fields, or when a kNN neighborhood structure over cells is needed for neighborhood-aggregated metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-construction))
- **knn-graph-construction-for-million-scale-scrnaseq**: Use when a scRNA-seq or CyTOF expression matrix exceeds ~100k cells (e.g. 1.3M-cell 10X mouse brain data) and a neighbourhood structure is needed for triplet-based dimensionality reduction where scikit-learn Barnes-Hut t-SNE would not finish within 24 hours. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-construction-for-million-scale-scrnaseq))
- **knn-graph-construction-from-cell-embeddings**: Use when paired scRNA-seq/scATAC-seq data need a cell-state DAG (not just a clustering or trajectory) so that Granger causal locus-gene inference can be run over an acyclic cell ordering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-construction-from-cell-embeddings))
- **knn-graph-construction-from-cophenetic-distances**: Use when you have a single-cell lineage tree (e.g., Cassiopeia Newick with cells as leaves) plus expression data and need a cell-neighborhood metric for phylogenetic autocorrelation with Hotspot. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-construction-from-cophenetic-distances))
- **knn-graph-construction-from-embeddings**: Use when you need a subpopulation-aware network over single-cell RNA-seq data (e.g., hematopoietic progenitor snapshots) so that trajectory search between any chosen source and target subpopulations, including rare cell types, can be performed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-construction-from-embeddings))
- **knn-graph-construction-from-pca-embeddings**: Use when you need a cell-cell similarity graph for label propagation or GNN classification on scRNA-seq data, and cells are represented as PCA-reduced expression embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-construction-from-pca-embeddings))
- **knn-graph-construction-from-similarity-matrix**: Use when a precomputed cell-by-cell similarity matrix (e.g., EDGE's averaged weak-learner matrix S) must be converted into a k-NNG before spectral embedding or graph-based dimensionality reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-construction-from-similarity-matrix))
- **knn-graph-construction-on-single-cell-data**: Use when you have a pre-processed N-cell × D-gene expression matrix and need a cell-to-cell transition probability graph (Markov matrix) as input to random walk or diffusion-based trajectory methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-construction-on-single-cell-data))
- **knn-graph-edge-construction**: Use when a labeled single-cell or feature matrix must be turned into a graph whose edges capture both same-class proximity and cross-class density-gradient transitions prior to force-based 2D embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-edge-construction))
- **knn-graph-layout-visualization**: Use when you need a class-label-agnostic baseline visualization of single-cell expression data that mixes clusters, to contrast with class-informed graph embeddings like GraphDDP. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-layout-visualization))
- **knn-graph-leiden-clustering**: Use when cell/gene feature embeddings (e.g. from tGPT) must be clustered into groups and quantitatively compared with known cell type annotations using NMI, ARI, and FMI metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-leiden-clustering))
- **knn-graph-leiden-clustering-single-cell**: Use when you have extracted per-cell latent factors from a multi-omics integration model (e.g. scMoMaT extract_cell_factors) and need discrete cluster labels plus a 2D UMAP visualization to interpret populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-leiden-clustering-single-cell))
- **knn-graph-post-processing**: Use when you have extracted cell factors (zs) from a first-stage scMoMaT model and need a KNN graph plus Leiden cluster labels for benchmarking (NMI/ARI/GC) or for second-stage retraining. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-post-processing))
- **knn-graph-precision-evaluation**: Decision support for choosing an ANN algorithm when building k-NN overlap graphs from sparse, dimensionality-reduced long-read embeddings and needing quantitative precision-vs-runtime rankings rather than a single default method. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/knn-graph-precision-evaluation))
- **knn-graph-trajectory-path-finding**: Use when snapshot scRNA-seq data (with scVelo/velocyto spliced-unspliced velocity) contains multiple or branching trajectories whose direction and topology cannot be resolved by expression-only TI methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-graph-trajectory-path-finding))
- **knn-imputation**: Use when filtering genes with high missingness and then imputing missing values in a bulk expression matrix with group-aware KNN through DMwR2, where donor samples are restricted by one annotation column before imputation. For strata with 10 or fewer samples, the script falls back to row-wise direct filling with mean or median. NOT for: single-cell data, multi-column stratification, non-tabular inputs, network access, or interactive workflows. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome-med-research-skills/Data%20Analysis/knn-imputation) · ★2.1k · 2 copies)
- **knn-imputation-benchmarking**: Use when comparing imputation methods for highly sparse APA ratio (RUD) matrices from spatially barcoded ST data, e.g. deciding whether stAPAminer's k-nearest-neighbor imputation or scDaPars better recovers APA signals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-imputation-benchmarking))
- **knn-imputation-feature-table**: Use when a metabolomics feature table (e.g. the cancerCell MetaboReport demo dataset) contains missing values from MNAR (below-detection-limit) or MAR mechanisms and you must restore a complete, NA-free intensity matrix before statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/knn-imputation-feature-table))
- **knn-imputation-for-metabolomics**: Use when a metabolomics concentration table (CSV/.xlsx uploaded to Viime) contains missing values heuristically classified as MCAR per metabolite per group, and a neighbor-based imputation is preferred over missForest, mean, or median. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/knn-imputation-for-metabolomics))
- **knn-imputation-method-comparison**: Decision support for choosing among KNN imputation algorithms when a metabolomics dataset has missing values (MVs) arising from both MNAR (below LOD) and MAR mechanisms, and RMSE/MLCI comparisons are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/knn-imputation-method-comparison))
- **knn-imputation-of-missing-xlogp3**: Use when a per-metabolite permeability or rule-based property classification requires XLogP3, but the descriptor is missing for the large majority of PubChem-mapped metabolite identifiers and correlated descriptors (MW, TPSA, HBD, HBA, RB) are available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/knn-imputation-of-missing-xlogp3))
- **knn-imputation-quality-assessment**: Use when after deciding to use KNN imputation on a metabolomic assay matrix with missing values, but before proceeding to normalization and statistical testing. Trigger conditions include: (1) your metabolomic dataset contains metabolites with varying degrees of missingness across samples; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/knn-imputation-quality-assessment))
- **knn-kernel-construction-from-marker-genes**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset contains unwanted biological variation (e.g., cell cycle, sex) that can be encoded by curated marker gene lists, and you need a cell-cell similarity kernel for SiFT gene-level filtering or covariate scores for regress_out/scVI baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-kernel-construction-from-marker-genes))
- **knn-kernel-construction-in-harmonized-pca-space**: Use when filtering a biological signal from scRNA-seq data via reference mapping — i.e., disease cells must be linked to their nearest healthy reference cells before projection-and-subtraction with SiFT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-kernel-construction-in-harmonized-pca-space))
- **knn-label-propagation-to-full-dataset**: Use when clusters or labels have been assigned only to a sketched subset of cells (e.g., Sphetcher spherical or geometric sketch) and you need to infer labels for all cells of the original expression/PCA matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-label-propagation-to-full-dataset))
- **knn-label-transfer-annotation-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when comparing reference mapping methods (e.g. ProjectSVR, Seurat, scArches, Symphony, ProjecTILs) on reference/query pairs where query cells have manually curated labels and you need accuracy/ARI benchmark numbers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-label-transfer-annotation-accuracy-evaluation))
- **knn-label-transfer-evaluation**: Use when paired TCR+transcriptome query single-cell studies must be annotated by mapping them into a pre-trained mvTCR reference embedding, and accuracy of kNN label transfer for metadata labels (e.g. cancer source, tissue type) must be quantified against a baseline projection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-label-transfer-evaluation--8238ddc3046bbb39))
- **knn-label-transfer-evaluation**: Use when you have uniPort latent embeddings from two heterogeneous single-cell datasets (e.g. scATAC gene activity vs scRNA) and need quantitative ARI+NMI+F1 and Silhouette metrics to judge integration quality, including unbalanced-matching cases where cell types are missing from one modality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-label-transfer-evaluation--47de85664778dea5))
- **knn-label-transfer-large-dataset**: Use when the scRNA-seq matrix exceeds scCAN's 5,000-cell training threshold and full network fusion on all cells is impractical, but per-cell cluster assignments are still needed for every cell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-label-transfer-large-dataset))
- **knn-label-transfer-to-reference-atlas**: Use when new query datasets have been integrated onto an annotated reference (e.g., a STACAS seed map) and cell subtype labels must be propagated to unlabeled cells using nearest-neighbor voting in the shared embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-label-transfer-to-reference-atlas))
- **knn-local-weighting-of-networks**: Use when multiple similarity matrices over the same node set (e.g., ONF-based and GIP kernel-based host similarities) must be locally sparsified to K neighbors before SNF-style fusion into a single similarity network. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/knn-local-weighting-of-networks))
- **knn-missing-value-imputation**: Use when an input gene-expression or methylation matrix (e.g., from golubEsets, ALL, or recount2) contains missing values (NAs) that must be filled before feature selection and consensus partitioning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/knn-missing-value-imputation))
- **knn-missing-value-imputation**: Use when an extracted LC-MS feature table still contains missing values after noisy features (&gt;20% MVs in QC samples or &gt;50% MVs across study groups) have been removed, and a complete intensity matrix is needed for statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/knn-missing-value-imputation))
- **knn-missing-value-imputation**: Use when a spot-by-poly(A)-site RUD (relative usage difference) matrix derived from spatially barcoded ST data is too sparse for downstream clustering or spatial-pattern tests, and a companion gene-spot count matrix is available to define spot neighborhoods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-missing-value-imputation))
- **knn-missing-value-imputation-peak-table**: Use when a DIMS/LC-MS peak table (X matrix of sample × m/z intensities) still contains missing values after Blank Filtering, Sample Filtering, Replicate Filtering and probabilistic quotient normalization, and a complete matrix is required before G-log transformation and PCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/knn-missing-value-imputation-peak-table))
- **knn-nearest-neighbor-distance-calculation**: Use when quantifying how closely inferred (semi-profiled) single-cell data matches real-profiled ground truth across representative counts, e.g. benchmarking scSemiProfiler against a selection-only baseline that merely copies each representative's cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-nearest-neighbor-distance-calculation))
- **knn-nearest-neighbor-matching-for-spot-correspondence**: Use when when integrating two spatial omics modalities (ST and SM) measured on the same tissue sample but at different spatial resolutions or spot coordinates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/knn-nearest-neighbor-matching-for-spot-correspondence))
- **knn-neighbor-graph-construction**: Use when a velocity or trajectory pipeline needs a cell-neighborhood graph over preprocessed scRNA-seq AnnData (e.g. after scvelo pp.filter_and_normalize with top-2000 highly variable genes) to support moment smoothing or local averaging. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-neighbor-graph-construction))
- **knn-neighbor-graph-construction-scrna-seq**: Use when a scRNA-seq expression matrix yields a sparse cell-to-cell similarity (e.g., from ADMM-solved sparse subspace representation) whose zero entries must be imputed within K-nearest-neighbor neighborhoods to obtain a dense similarity for spectral clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-neighbor-graph-construction-scrna-seq))
- **knn-neighbor-voting-label-propagation**: Use when annotating individual cells directly from marker GEPs (no clustering) and you need to smooth/consolidate per-cell GEP assignments using a precomputed KNN neighbor index. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-neighbor-voting-label-propagation))
- **knn-neighborhood-graph-construction**: Use when preparing single-cell or spatially resolved multi-omics AnnData objects for SSGATE training and you must build cell-cell neighborhood graphs from expression profiles ('X_pca') or spatial coordinates ('spatial'). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/knn-neighborhood-graph-construction))
- **knn-neighborhood-preservation-assessment**: Use when comparing two embedding configurations (e.g., t-SNE perplexity 100 from scDEED vs 1000 from EMBEDR) by measuring retention of pre-embedding neighbor structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-neighborhood-preservation-assessment))
- **knn-neighborhood-score-computation**: Use when comparing embedding methods on a multi-sample single-cell dataset for batch-effect correction versus biological signal retention, e.g. scoring SCING module AUCell embeddings against FastMNN/Harmony/Seurat on snRNA-seq cell annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-neighborhood-score-computation))
- **knn-parameter-sensitivity-analysis**: Use when validating that a graph-based trajectory-inference result is robust to the KNN hyperparameter on time-labeled single-cell data (e.g., ~90K-cell CyTOF panels with day annotations) by correlating inferred pseudotime against known sampling days across a KNN sweep. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-parameter-sensitivity-analysis))
- **knn-protein-abundance-inference**: Use when a query dataset measures only mRNA (no CITE-seq ADT counts) but you need surface protein levels, and a multimodal CITE-seq reference atlas with a joint mRNA/protein embedding is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-protein-abundance-inference))
- **knn-purity-assessment**: Use when benchmarking trajectory inference methods (e.g. Monocle, Slingshot, dynverse MST) on simulated continuous single-cell populations where gold-standard branch labels are available and ordering-only metrics are insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/knn-purity-assessment))
- **knn-recommender**: Panduan dan standar dalam mengimplementasikan, memodifikasi, dan mengoptimalkan sistem rekomendasi produk K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN) di proyek Fordza Web. Gunakan skill ini setiap kali user meminta penyesuaian pada algoritma KNN, matriks fitur produk, penghitungan jarak Euclidean, atau endpoint antarmuka katalog produk yang menggunakan fitur rekomendasi.
